Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the schrödinger object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 599. The moduli space has dimension at most 894 with 2 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to schrödinger theory, we decompose the problem into 599 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 894. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
